      box-shadow: 0 -10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.8); transform: translateY(100%); transition: transform 0.3s ease;
    }
    .batch-toolbar.active { transform: translateY(0); }
    .select-checkbox { width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: var(--gold); cursor: pointer; display: none; }
    .select-mode-active .select-checkbox { display: inline-block; }

    .btn-delete {
      background: rgba(224,92,92,0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(224,92,92,0.3); color: var(--red);
      padding: 5px 12px; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 0.68rem; font-weight: 700;
      font-family: 'DM Mono', monospace; cursor: pointer; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; margin-top: 10px;
    }
    .btn-delete:hover { background: var(--red); color: #fff; }

    /* Loading Overlay */
    #global-loading-overlay {
      position: fixed; inset: 0; background: rgba(10, 10, 15, 0.75); backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
      z-index: 9999; display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px;
    }
    .spinner {
      width: 36px; height: 36px; border: 3px solid rgba(201,168,76,0.2); border-top-color: var(--gold);
      border-radius: 50%; animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite;
    }
    @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }

<body id="body-root">

<div id="global-loading-overlay">
  <div class="spinner"></div>
  <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; color:var(--gold); font-size:0.85rem; letter-spacing:0.08em;" id="loading-text">Processing request...</div>
</div>

<div class="main-wrap">
  <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:16px; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:10px;">
    <div>
      <h2 style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; font-size:1.25rem; font-weight:800; color:var(--gold);">Missionaries Roster</h2>
      <p style="font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--muted); margin-top:4px;">
        Showing <span id="showing-count">0</span> of <span id="roster-count">0</span> registered missionaries.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex; gap:8px;">
      <button class="filter-btn" id="toggle-select-mode" onclick="toggleSelectMode()">☑️ Select Mode: OFF</button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="table-controls">
    <input class="search-input" id="search-box" placeholder="Search missionary name, email, or code..."/>
    <button class="filter-btn active" onclick="filterType('all', this)">All</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="filterType('elder', this)">Elders (24M)</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="filterType('sister', this)">Sisters (18M)</button>
    
    <select class="sort-select" id="sort-select" onchange="handleSortChange(this.value)">
      <option value="name_asc">Sort: Name (A-Z)</option>
      <option value="name_desc">Sort: Name (Z-A)</option>
      <option value="points_desc">Sort: Points (High to Low)</option>
      <option value="points_asc">Sort: Points (Low to High)</option>
      <option value="oldest">Sort: Oldest Added</option>
      <option value="newest">Sort: Newest Added</option>
    </select>
  </div>

  <div class="roster-list" id="roster-container">
    <div style="text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.8rem;">Loading missionaries...</div>
  </div>

  <div class="pagination-bar">
    <button class="page-btn" id="prev-page-btn" onclick="changePage(-1)">← Previous</button>
    <span id="page-indicator">Page 1</span>
    <button class="page-btn" id="next-page-btn" onclick="changePage(1)">Next →</button>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Floating Batch Toolbar -->
<div class="batch-toolbar" id="batch-toolbar">
  <div style="display:flex; gap:12px; align-items:center;">
    <span style="font-size:0.78rem; color:var(--gold); font-weight:bold;" id="selected-count-label">0 Selected</span>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="selectAllVisible()">Select All</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="selectCurrentCohort()">Select Current Cohort</button>
    <button class="filter-btn" onclick="deselectAll()">Deselect</button>
  </div>
  <div style="display:flex; gap:10px;">
    <button class="filter-btn" style="background:var(--gold-dim); color:var(--gold); border-color:var(--gold);" onclick="promptBulkAddPoints()">⭐ Bulk Add Points</button>
    <button class="btn-delete" style="margin-top:0;" onclick="promptBulkDelete()">🗑️ Bulk Delete Selected</button>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
let state = {
  missionaries: [],
  filteredList: [],
  currentFilter: 'all',
  currentSort: 'name_asc',
  searchQuery: '',
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 50,
  selectMode: false,
  selectedEmails: new Set()
};

function showLoading(msg = "Processing request...") {
  document.getElementById('loading-text').textContent = msg;
  document.getElementById('global-loading-overlay').style.display = 'flex';
}

function hideLoading() {
  document.getElementById('global-loading-overlay').style.display = 'none';
}

function debounce(func, wait = 250) {
  let timeout;
  return function(...args) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    timeout = setTimeout(() => func.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}

function loadRoster() {
  const cached = LocalStore.get('missionaries', []);
  if (cached.length > 0) {
    state.missionaries = cached;
    document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = cached.length.toLocaleString();
    applyFilterAndRender();
  } else {
    triggerGlobalRefresh();
  }
}

window.onPageDataRefreshed = () => {
  state.missionaries = LocalStore.get('missionaries', []);
  document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
  applyFilterAndRender();
};

function toggleSelectMode() {
  state.selectMode = !state.selectMode;
  const btn = document.getElementById('toggle-select-mode');
  const toolbar = document.getElementById('batch-toolbar');
  const root = document.getElementById('body-root');

  if (state.selectMode) {
    btn.textContent = "☑️ Select Mode: ON";
    btn.classList.add("active");
    root.classList.add("select-mode-active");
    toolbar.classList.add("active");
  } else {
    btn.textContent = "☑️ Select Mode: OFF";
    btn.classList.remove("active");
    root.classList.remove("select-mode-active");
    toolbar.classList.remove("active");
    state.selectedEmails.clear();
    updateSelectedCount();
  }
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

function selectAllVisible() {
  state.filteredList.forEach(m => state.selectedEmails.add(m.email.toLowerCase()));
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function selectCurrentCohort() {
  state.filteredList.forEach(m => {
    const isSister = (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister');
    if (state.currentFilter === 'all' || (state.currentFilter === 'sister' && isSister) || (state.currentFilter === 'elder' && !isSister)) {
      state.selectedEmails.add(m.email.toLowerCase());
    }
  });
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function deselectAll() {
  state.selectedEmails.clear();
  renderPaginatedCards();
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function toggleCheckbox(email, e) {
  e.stopPropagation();
  const cleanEmail = email.toLowerCase();
  if (state.selectedEmails.has(cleanEmail)) {
    state.selectedEmails.delete(cleanEmail);
  } else {
    state.selectedEmails.add(cleanEmail);
  }
  updateSelectedCount();
}

function updateSelectedCount() {
  document.getElementById('selected-count-label').textContent = `${state.selectedEmails.size} Selected`;
}

function filterType(type, btn) {
  state.currentFilter = type;
  state.page = 1;
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(b => {
    if (!b.id.includes('toggle-select-mode')) b.classList.remove('active');
  });
  btn.classList.add('active');
  applyFilterAndRender();
}

function handleSortChange(sortVal) {
  state.currentSort = sortVal;
  state.page = 1;
  applyFilterAndRender();
}

const handleDebouncedSearch = debounce((val) => {
  state.searchQuery = val.toLowerCase().trim();
  state.page = 1;
  applyFilterAndRender();
});

document.getElementById('search-box').addEventListener('input', (e) => handleDebouncedSearch(e.target.value));

function applyFilterAndRender() {
  let list = [...state.missionaries];

  if (state.currentFilter === 'elder') {
    list = list.filter(m => (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('elder') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('elder'));
  } else if (state.currentFilter === 'sister') {
    list = list.filter(m => (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister'));
  }

  if (state.searchQuery) {
    list = list.filter(m =>
      (m.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery) ||
      (m.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery) ||
      (m.referral_code || '').toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery)
    );
  }

  list.sort((a, b) => {
    if (state.currentSort === 'name_asc') {
      return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'name_desc') {
      return (b.name || '').localeCompare(a.name || '');
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'points_desc') {
      return (Number(b.points) || 0) - (Number(a.points) || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'points_asc') {
      return (Number(a.points) || 0) - (Number(b.points) || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'oldest') {
      return (a.rowid || a.id || 0) - (b.rowid || b.id || 0);
    } else if (state.currentSort === 'newest') {
      return (b.rowid || b.id || 0) - (a.rowid || a.id || 0);
    }
    return 0;
  });

  state.filteredList = list;
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

function changePage(delta) {
  state.page += delta;
  renderPaginatedCards();
}

async function deleteMissionary(email, name) {
  if (!confirm(`Permanently remove ${name} (${email}) from database?`)) return;

  showLoading(`Deleting ${name}...`);
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete_missionary", email })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Removed ${name} from roster.`);
      state.missionaries = state.missionaries.filter(m => m.email.toLowerCase() !== email.toLowerCase());
      LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
      document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
      applyFilterAndRender();
    } else {
      showToast(data.error || "Failed to delete missionary", "error");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Network error: " + err.message, "error");
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
}

async function promptBulkAddPoints() {
  if (state.selectedEmails.size === 0) {
    showToast("No missionaries selected.", "error");
    return;
  }
  const pts = prompt(`Enter points to add (or subtract using negative numbers) for ${state.selectedEmails.size} selected missionaries:`, "5");
  if (pts === null) return;
  const delta = parseInt(pts, 10);
  if (isNaN(delta)) {
    showToast("Invalid number.", "error");
    return;
  }

  showLoading(`Updating points for ${state.selectedEmails.size} missionaries...`);
  let successCount = 0;
  for (const email of state.selectedEmails) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: "update_missionary_points", email, delta })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) {
        successCount++;
        const m = state.missionaries.find(x => x.email.toLowerCase() === email);
        if (m) m.points = Math.max(0, (Number(m.points) || 0) + delta);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  hideLoading();
  LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
  applyFilterAndRender();
  showToast(`Successfully updated points for ${successCount} missionaries!`);
}

async function promptBulkDelete() {
  if (state.selectedEmails.size === 0) {
    showToast("No missionaries selected.", "error");
    return;
  }
  if (!confirm(`Permanently delete ${state.selectedEmails.size} selected missionaries from the database?`)) return;

  showLoading(`Bulk deleting ${state.selectedEmails.size} missionaries...`);
  let successCount = 0;
  for (const email of state.selectedEmails) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete_missionary", email })
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.ok) successCount++;
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  hideLoading();
  state.missionaries = state.missionaries.filter(m => !state.selectedEmails.has(m.email.toLowerCase()));
  state.selectedEmails.clear();
  LocalStore.set('missionaries', state.missionaries);
  document.getElementById('roster-count').textContent = state.missionaries.length.toLocaleString();
  updateSelectedCount();
  applyFilterAndRender();
  showToast(`Successfully deleted ${successCount} missionaries.`);
}

function renderPaginatedCards() {
  const totalItems = state.filteredList.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalItems / state.pageSize) || 1;
  state.page = Math.max(1, Math.min(state.page, totalPages));

  const startIdx = (state.page - 1) * state.pageSize;
  const pageItems = state.filteredList.slice(startIdx, startIdx + state.pageSize);

  document.getElementById('showing-count').textContent = totalItems > 0 ? `${startIdx + 1}-${Math.min(startIdx + state.pageSize, totalItems)}` : '0';
  document.getElementById('page-indicator').textContent = `Page ${state.page} of ${totalPages}`;
  document.getElementById('prev-page-btn').disabled = state.page <= 1;
  document.getElementById('next-page-btn').disabled = state.page >= totalPages;

  const container = document.getElementById('roster-container');
  if (!pageItems.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center; padding:30px; color:var(--muted); font-size:0.8rem;">No matching missionaries found.</div>';
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = pageItems.map(m => {
    const isSister = (m.cohort || '').toLowerCase().includes('sister') || (m.name || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('sister');
    const totalMonths = Number(m.max_months) || (isSister ? 18 : 24);
    const sentCount = Math.min(Number(m.months_sent) || 0, totalMonths);
    const pct = Math.min(Math.round((sentCount / totalMonths) * 100), 100);
    const isChecked = state.selectedEmails.has(m.email.toLowerCase()) ? 'checked' : '';

    return `
      <div class="missionary-card">
        <div class="card-main-row">
          <input type="checkbox" class="select-checkbox" ${isChecked} onclick="toggleCheckbox('${m.email}', event)"/>
          <div class="card-summary" onclick="this.closest('.missionary-card').classList.toggle('open')">
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
              <span class="cohort-pill ${isSister ? 'cohort-sister' : 'cohort-elder'}">${isSister ? 'Sister' : 'Elder'}</span>
              <span style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; font-size:0.85rem; font-weight:700;">${m.name || 'Unnamed'}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px;">
              <span style="color:var(--gold); font-weight:bold; background:var(--gold-dim); padding:2px 8px; border-radius:4px; font-size:0.7rem;">${m.points || 0} PTS</span>
              <span style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.7rem;">▼</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card-details">
          <div>✉️ <strong>Email:</strong> ${m.email}</div>
          <div>🏷️ <strong>Referral Code:</strong> ${m.referral_code || 'N/A'}</div>
          <div>🆔 <strong>Messenger PSID:</strong> ${m.psid || '<span style="color:var(--muted)">Not Linked</span>'}</div>
          <div>⚡ <strong>Status:</strong> <span style="text-transform:uppercase; color:${m.status === 'active' ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)'}">${m.status || 'active'}</span></div>

          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; font-size:0.65rem; color:var(--muted); margin-top:8px;">
            <span>📩 ${sentCount} / ${totalMonths} Drip Letters</span>
            <span>${pct}%</span>
          </div>
          <div style="width:100%; height:5px; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border-radius:99px; overflow:hidden; margin-top:3px;">
            <div style="height:100%; width:${pct}%; background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--green),var(--gold));"></div>
          </div>

          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:flex-end;">
            <button class="btn-delete" onclick="deleteMissionary('${m.email}', '${m.name || 'Missionary'}')">🗑️ Delete Missionary</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('missionaries', 'Missionaries Roster');
  loadRoster();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e

<script>
let currentPowerState = "offline";
let captchaRound = 1;

const CAPTCHA_IMAGES = [
  { id: "correct", src: "https://i.postimg.cc/mgjRv9Dy/IMG-7154.jpg", isHandsome: true },
  { id: "wrong1", src: "https://i.postimg.cc/9X92WK05/images.webp", isHandsome: false },
  { id: "wrong2", src: "https://i.postimg.cc/s21Fb0qk/images-(1).webp", isHandsome: false }
];

function initMonthPicker() {
  const sel = document.getElementById("test-drip-month");
  if (!sel) return;
  sel.innerHTML = "";
  for (let i = 1; i <= 24; i++) {
    const opt = document.createElement("option");
    opt.value = i;
    opt.textContent = `Month ${i}`;
    sel.appendChild(opt);
  }
}

function toggleMonthPicker(val) {
  const pickerWrap = document.getElementById("month-picker-wrap");
  if (pickerWrap) {
    pickerWrap.style.display = val === "drip" ? "block" : "none";
  }
}

async function loadSettings() {
  const btn = document.getElementById("power-toggle-btn");
  if (!btn) return;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=health_check");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      currentPowerState = "online";
      btn.className = "btn btn-gold";
      btn.textContent = "⚡ System: ONLINE (Click to Sleep)";
    } else {
      currentPowerState = "offline";
      btn.className = "btn btn-dark";
      btn.textContent = "💤 System: OFFLINE / SLEEPING (Click to Wake)";
    }
  } catch(e) {
    btn.className = "btn btn-dark";
    btn.textContent = "⚠️ Disconnected";
  }

  loadCdnSettings();
}

async function loadCdnSettings() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_cdn_config");
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.ok && d.config) {
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-owner")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-owner").value = d.config.cdn_github_owner || "";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-repo")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-repo").value = d.config.cdn_github_repo || "";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-branch")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-branch").value = d.config.cdn_github_branch || "main";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-path")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-path").value = d.config.cdn_upload_path || "assets/rewards";
      if (document.getElementById("set-cdn-token")) document.getElementById("set-cdn-token").value = d.config.cdn_github_token || "";
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

async function saveCdnSettings() {
  const payload = {
    cdn_github_owner: document.getElementById("set-cdn-owner").value.trim(),
    cdn_github_repo: document.getElementById("set-cdn-repo").value.trim(),
    cdn_github_branch: document.getElementById("set-cdn-branch").value.trim() || "main",
    cdn_upload_path: document.getElementById("set-cdn-path").value.trim() || "assets/rewards",
    cdn_github_token: document.getElementById("set-cdn-token").value.trim()
  };

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=save_cdn_config", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("jsDelivr & GitHub variables saved!");
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Failed to save CDN variables", "error");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Error saving: " + err.message, "error");
  }
}

function startPowerCaptcha() {
  captchaRound = 1;
  renderCaptchaRound();
  document.getElementById("captcha-modal").style.display = "flex";
}

function closeCaptchaModal() {
  document.getElementById("captcha-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function renderCaptchaRound() {
  const arena = document.getElementById("captcha-arena-container");
  const title = document.getElementById("captcha-round-title");
  const diffLabel = document.getElementById("captcha-difficulty-label");

  document.getElementById("p-dot-1").className = captchaRound >= 1 ? "progress-dot active" : "progress-dot";
  document.getElementById("p-dot-2").className = captchaRound >= 2 ? "progress-dot active" : "progress-dot";
  document.getElementById("p-dot-3").className = captchaRound >= 3 ? "progress-dot active" : "progress-dot";

  let animClasses = ["", "", ""];
  if (captchaRound === 1) {
    title.innerHTML = `Round 1 of 3: Tap the real <strong>Handsome Face</strong> (Static)`;
    diffLabel.textContent = "Difficulty: Level 1 (Easy)";
  } else if (captchaRound === 2) {
    title.innerHTML = `Round 2 of 3: Target is <strong>Moving!</strong> (Medium)`;
    diffLabel.textContent = "Difficulty: Level 2 (Medium)";
    animClasses = ["move-float-1", "move-float-2", "move-float-3"];
  } else if (captchaRound === 3) {
    title.innerHTML = `Round 3 of 3: <strong>Fast Speed & Orbit!</strong> (Hard)`;
    diffLabel.textContent = "Difficulty: Level 3 (Hard)";
    animClasses = ["move-fast-1", "move-fast-2", "move-fast-3"];
  }

  const shuffled = [...CAPTCHA_IMAGES].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);

  arena.innerHTML = shuffled.map((item, idx) => `
    <div class="captcha-card ${animClasses[idx]}" onclick="handleCaptchaPick(${item.isHandsome})">
      <img src="${item.src}" alt="Face">
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

async function handleCaptchaPick(isHandsome) {
  const box = document.getElementById("captcha-box");

  if (!isHandsome) {
    box.classList.remove("shake-box");
    void box.offsetWidth;
    box.classList.add("shake-box");
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast("Wrong face selected! Resetting to Round 1.", "error");
    }
    captchaRound = 1;
    renderCaptchaRound();
    return;
  }

  if (captchaRound < 3) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast(`✓ Round ${captchaRound} Passed! Speed increasing...`);
    }
    captchaRound++;
    renderCaptchaRound();
  } else {
    closeCaptchaModal();
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast("🏆 All 3 Rounds Complete! Pogi Certified.");
    }
    await executePowerToggle();
  }
}

async function executePowerToggle() {
  const nextState = currentPowerState === "online" ? "offline" : "online";
  const btn = document.getElementById("power-toggle-btn");
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Saving to Turso...";
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "toggle_power", state: nextState })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
        showToast(`Master power state changed to ${nextState.toUpperCase()}`);
      }
      loadSettings();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast("Failed to update power state.", "error");
    }
    loadSettings();
  }
}

async function sendTestEmail() {
  const input = document.getElementById("test-email-input").value.trim();
  const templateType = document.getElementById("test-template-select").value;
  const month = document.getElementById("test-drip-month").value;
  const consoleBox = document.getElementById("test-console");
  const btn = document.getElementById("btn-send-test");

  if (!input) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Enter at least one email address.", "error");
    return;
  }

  consoleBox.style.display = "block";
  consoleBox.textContent = `⏳ Dispatching authentic [${templateType.toUpperCase()}] template from templates directory...\nSender: noreply.timelesscreations.ph@gmail.com\nTarget(s): ${input}\n\n`;
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Sending...";
  btn.disabled = true;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: "test_email",
        email: input,
        template_type: templateType,
        month: Number(month) || 1
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    consoleBox.textContent += JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);

    if (data.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`Test dispatch successful! Delivered ${data.totalSent || 1} email(s).`);
      consoleBox.textContent += "\n\n✅ ALL DISPATCHES DELIVERED SUCCESSFULLY USING TEMPLATE FILE!";
    } else {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`Dispatch issue: ${data.error || "Review details below"}`, "error");
      consoleBox.textContent += "\n\n❌ ERROR: See diagnostic response details above.";
    }
  } catch (err) {
    consoleBox.textContent += "\n\n❌ Network Error: " + err.message;
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Network execution error.", "error");
  } finally {
    btn.textContent = "🚀 Dispatch Authentic Template";
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
}

function openCronModal() {
  document.getElementById("modal-input-val").value = "";
  document.getElementById("custom-modal").style.display = "flex";
}
function closeModal() {
  document.getElementById("custom-modal").style.display = "none";
}
function submitCronModal() {
  const val = document.getElementById("modal-input-val").value.trim();
  if (val === "Pogi si Allen") {
    closeModal();
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Executing scheduled drip cron...");
    fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "force_cron" })
    }).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Cron execution completed successfully!");
    });
  } else {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Security phrase did not match.", "error");
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') {
    initAppLayout('settings', 'System Control Room');
  }
  initMonthPicker();
  loadSettings();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e

    <div class="modal-content">
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:16px;">
            <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif; font-size:1.1rem; font-weight:bold; color:var(--gold);" id="modal-heading">Add Reward Item</div>
            <button onclick="closeProductModal()" style="background:none; border:none; color:var(--muted); font-size:1.4rem; cursor:pointer;">✕</button>
        </div>
        <input type="hidden" id="modal-item-index">
        <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">Reward Item Name</label>
            <input type="text" id="modal-prod-name" class="form-input" placeholder="e.g. Temple Keychain">
        </div>
        <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">Cost in Points</label>
            <input type="number" id="modal-prod-price" class="form-input" placeholder="e.g. 6">
        </div>
        <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">Image URL (1:1 Ratio)</label>
            <input type="url" id="modal-prod-img" class="form-input" placeholder="https://i.postimg.cc/.../item.png" oninput="updateImagePreview(this.value)">
            <img id="modal-preview-box" class="img-preview-box" src="https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png" alt="1:1 Preview">
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:flex-end; gap:10px; margin-top:18px;">
            <button class="btn btn-dark" onclick="closeProductModal()">Cancel</button>
            <button class="btn btn-gold" onclick="applyModalChanges()">✓ Apply to List</button>
        </div>
    </div>

<div class="save-banner" id="save-banner">
    <span style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--gold); font-weight:bold;">⚡ You have unsaved local edits</span>
    <button class="btn btn-gold" id="btn-sync-turso" onclick="saveAllToTurso()">💾 Save Changes to Turso</button>

<script>
let localItems = [];

function markDirty() {
  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "flex";
  LocalStore.set('temp_products', localItems);
}

async function loadInitialData(forceFetch = false) {
  const cached = LocalStore.get('temp_products', null);
  if (cached && !forceFetch) {
    localItems = cached;
    renderGrid();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_products&type=reward");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      localItems = data.products || [];
      LocalStore.set('temp_products', localItems);
      renderGrid();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    document.getElementById("products-grid").innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">Error loading items.</div>';
  }
}

function renderGrid() {
  const grid = document.getElementById("products-grid");
  if (!localItems.length) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--muted); font-size:0.75rem;">No reward products. Click "+ Add New Reward" above to create one.</div>';
    return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = localItems.map((p, idx) => `
    <div class="card" style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px; margin-bottom:0;">
      <div class="card-img-wrap">
        <img src="${p.image_url || 'https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png'}" alt="${p.name}">
      </div>
      <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size:0.85rem; margin-top:4px;">${p.name}</div>
      <div style="color:var(--gold); font-size:0.72rem; font-weight:bold;">⭐ ${p.price || 0} PTS</div>
      <div style="display:flex; gap:6px; margin-top:6px;">
        <button class="btn btn-dark" style="flex:1;" onclick="openProductModal(${idx})">✏️ Edit</button>
        <button class="btn btn-red" onclick="deleteLocalItem(${idx})">🗑️</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function updateImagePreview(url) {
  document.getElementById("modal-preview-box").src = url.trim() || 'https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png';
}

function openProductModal(idx = null) {
  if (idx !== null && localItems[idx]) {
    const p = localItems[idx];
    document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value = idx;
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value = p.name || "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value = p.price || 0;
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = p.image_url || "";
    updateImagePreview(p.image_url || "");
    document.getElementById("modal-heading").textContent = "Edit Reward Item";
  } else {
    document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value = "";
    document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value = "";
    updateImagePreview("");
    document.getElementById("modal-heading").textContent = "Add New Reward Item";
  }
  document.getElementById("product-modal").style.display = "flex";
}

function closeProductModal() {
  document.getElementById("product-modal").style.display = "none";
}

function applyModalChanges() {
  const idx = document.getElementById("modal-item-index").value;
  const name = document.getElementById("modal-prod-name").value.trim();
  const price = parseInt(document.getElementById("modal-prod-price").value, 10) || 0;
  const image_url = document.getElementById("modal-prod-img").value.trim();

  if (!name) {
    showToast("Reward item name is required.", "error");
    return;
  }

  if (idx !== "") {
    localItems[Number(idx)] = { name, price, image_url };
    showToast(`Updated "${name}" in local list.`);
  } else {
    localItems.push({ name, price, image_url });
    showToast(`Added "${name}" to local list.`);
  }

  closeProductModal();
  renderGrid();
  markDirty();
}

function deleteLocalItem(idx) {
  const removed = localItems.splice(idx, 1);
  renderGrid();
  markDirty();
  showToast(`Removed "${removed[0]?.name || 'Item'}" from list.`);
}

async function saveAllToTurso() {
  const btn = document.getElementById("btn-sync-turso");
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Syncing to Turso...";
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ 
        action: "sync_catalog", 
        type: "reward", 
        products: localItems 
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
      btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
      showToast("Reward catalog saved to Turso database!");
    } else {
      showToast(data.error || "Failed to save to Turso.", "error");
      btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Network error saving to Turso.", "error");
    btn.textContent = "💾 Save Changes to Turso";
  }
}

window.syncData = async () => {
  localStorage.removeItem('tcrp_temp_products');
  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
  await loadInitialData(true);
  showToast("Re-synced workspace with Turso database.");
};

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  initAppLayout('messengerbot', 'Messenger Bot Rewards');
  loadInitialData();
});
</script>

</body>
</html>
-e

            <th>Order ID</th>
            <th>Recipient</th>
            <th>Email</th>
            <th>Claimed Reward</th>
            <th>Points</th>
            <th style="text-align:center;">Status / Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="pending-claims-tbody">
          <tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--muted);">Loading pending claims...</td></tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- Cash Product Catalog Tab -->
  <div class="tab-pane" id="products-tab">
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:14px;">
        <h3>Cash POS Catalog (PHP Pricing)</h3>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="padding:4px 10px;font-size:0.65rem;" onclick="addNewProductSetting()">+ Add Product</button>
      </div>
      <table class="data-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Product Name</th>
            <th style="width:160px;">Price (PHP ₱)</th>
            <th style="width:100px;text-align:center;">Action</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="products-settings-tbody"></tbody>
      </table>
      <div style="margin-top:16px;">
        <button class="btn" id="save-products-btn" onclick="saveProductSettings()">💾 Save Cash Products to Turso</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

<script>
let missionaries = [];
let cashInvoices = [];
let orders = [];
let productCatalog = [];
let recipientMode = 'db';

function switchNavTab(tabId, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-pane').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add('active');
  btn.classList.add('active');
}

async function loadData() {
  try {
    const [mRes, invRes, prodRes] = await Promise.all([
      fetch('/api/main?action=get_missionaries'),
      fetch('/api/main?action=get_invoices'),
      fetch('/api/main?action=get_products&type=cash')
    ]);

    const mData = await mRes.json();
    const invData = await invRes.json();
    const prodData = await prodRes.json();

    missionaries = mData.missionaries || [];
    cashInvoices = invData.invoices || [];
    productCatalog = prodData.products || [];

    if (productCatalog.length === 0) {
      productCatalog = [
        { name: "Standard Missionary Strap", price: 150 },
        { name: "Customized Nametag", price: 250 },
        { name: "Scripture Cover Set", price: 450 }
      ];
    }

    populateMissionarySelect(missionaries);
    renderInvoicesTable();
    renderProductSettings();
    loadOrders();

    if (document.querySelectorAll('#items-tbody tr').length === 0 && productCatalog.length > 0) {
      addItemRow(productCatalog[0].name, productCatalog[0].price, 1);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showToast("Failed to load inventory data", "error");
  }
}

async function loadOrders() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=get_orders');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      orders = data.orders || [];
      renderPendingClaimsTable();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    showToast("Failed to load orders", "error");
  }
}

function renderPendingClaimsTable() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('pending-claims-tbody');
  const badge = document.getElementById('pending-claims-badge');
  const pending = orders.filter(o => (o.status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase() === 'PENDING');
  badge.textContent = pending.length;

  if (pending.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--muted);">No pending rewards claims found.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = pending.map(o => `
    <tr>
      <td style="font-weight:bold;color:var(--gold);">${o.order_id}</td>
      <td style="font-weight:600;">${o.name || 'Anonymous'}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--muted);">${o.email || '—'}</td>
      <td><strong>🎁 ${o.item}</strong> (Claimed Free)</td>
      <td><span style="color:var(--gold);font-weight:bold;">${o.points_cost} PTS</span></td>
      <td style="text-align:center;">
        <select class="status-select pending" onchange="updateClaimStatus('${o.order_id}', this.value)">
          <option value="PENDING" selected>PENDING</option>
          <option value="COMPLETED">MARK COMPLETE &amp; SEND THANKS</option>
        </select>
      </td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
}

async function updateClaimStatus(orderId, newStatus) {
  if (newStatus === 'COMPLETED' && !confirm(`Mark order ${orderId} as COMPLETED and automatically dispatch Thank You email to recipient?`)) {
    loadOrders();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'update_order_status', order_id: orderId, status: newStatus })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Order ${orderId} updated to ${newStatus}!`);
      loadOrders();
    } else {
      showToast("Failed to update status", "error");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Network error updating order", "error");
  }
}

function switchRecipientMode(mode) {
  recipientMode = mode;
  document.getElementById('mode-db-btn').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'db');
  document.getElementById('mode-custom-btn').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'custom');
  document.getElementById('recipient-db-box').style.display = mode === 'db' ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('recipient-custom-box').style.display = mode === 'custom' ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (mode === 'db') handleMissionarySelect();
  else handleCustomInput();
}

function handleDbPhoneInput() {
  document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = document.getElementById('db-phone-input').value.trim() || '—';
}

function handleCustomInput() {
  document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent = document.getElementById('custom-name-input').value.trim() || 'Customer [Name]';
  document.getElementById('prev-email').textContent = document.getElementById('custom-email-input').value.trim() || 'customer@example.com';
  document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = document.getElementById('custom-phone-input').value.trim() || '—';
}

function populateMissionarySelect(list) {
  const sel = document.getElementById('missionary-select');
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Choose Missionary --</option>' + 
    list.map(m => `<option value="${m.email}">${m.name} (${m.email})</option>`).join('');
}

function filterMissionaryDropdown(query) {
  const q = query.toLowerCase();
  populateMissionarySelect(missionaries.filter(m => (m.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (m.email || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
}

function handleMissionarySelect() {
  const m = missionaries.find(x => x.email === document.getElementById('missionary-select').value);
  if (m) {
    document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent = m.name;
    document.getElementById('prev-email').textContent = m.email;
    document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = document.getElementById('db-phone-input').value.trim() || '—';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent = "Elder / Sister [Name]";
    document.getElementById('prev-email').textContent = "customer@example.com";
    document.getElementById('prev-phone').textContent = "—";
  }
  recalculateTotal();
}

function addItemRow(defaultName = "", defaultPrice = 0, defaultQty = 1) {
  if (!defaultName && productCatalog.length > 0) {
    defaultName = productCatalog[0].name;
    defaultPrice = productCatalog[0].price;
  }
  const tbody = document.getElementById('items-tbody');
  const tr = document.createElement('tr');
  tr.className = 'item-row';
  tr.innerHTML = `
    <td>
      <select class="form-select item-select" onchange="handleProductChange(this)" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;">
        ${productCatalog.map(p => `<option value="${p.name}" data-price="${p.price}" ${p.name === defaultName ? 'selected' : ''}>${p.name} (₱${p.price})</option>`).join('')}
      </select>
    </td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input item-qty" value="${defaultQty}" min="1" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;text-align:center;" oninput="recalculateTotal()"/></td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input item-price" value="${defaultPrice}" min="0" style="padding:4px 6px;font-size:0.7rem;text-align:center;" oninput="recalculateTotal()"/></td>
    <td style="text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:var(--gold);font-size:0.75rem;" class="item-subtotal">₱${(defaultPrice * defaultQty).toFixed(2)}</td>
    <td style="text-align:center;"><button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove(); recalculateTotal();">✕</button></td>
  `;
  tbody.appendChild(tr);
  recalculateTotal();
}

function handleProductChange(selectEl) {
  const opt = selectEl.options[selectEl.selectedIndex];
  selectEl.closest('tr').querySelector('.item-price').value = opt.getAttribute('data-price') || 0;
  recalculateTotal();
}

function recalculateTotal() {
  let subtotal = 0;
  const previewItems = [];
  document.querySelectorAll('.item-row').forEach(r => {
    const name = r.querySelector('.item-select').value;
    const qty = parseInt(r.querySelector('.item-qty').value, 10) || 1;
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.item-price').value) || 0;
    const rowSubtotal = qty * price;
    r.querySelector('.item-subtotal').textContent = `₱${rowSubtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
    subtotal += rowSubtotal;
    previewItems.push({ name, qty, price, subtotal: rowSubtotal });
  });

  const discountType = document.getElementById('discount-type').value;
  const discountVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('discount-val').value) || 0;
  let discountDeducted = discountType === 'percent' ? (subtotal * (discountVal / 100)) : discountVal;
  if (discountDeducted > subtotal) discountDeducted = subtotal;

  const grandTotal = subtotal - discountDeducted;
  document.getElementById('calc-subtotal').textContent = `₱${subtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('calc-discount-deducted').textContent = `-₱${discountDeducted.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('calc-grand-total').textContent = `₱${grandTotal.toFixed(2)}`;

  document.getElementById('prev-subtotal').textContent = `₱${subtotal.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('prev-discount').textContent = `-₱${discountDeducted.toFixed(2)}`;
  document.getElementById('prev-total').textContent = `₱${grandTotal.toFixed(2)}`;

  const prevTbody = document.getElementById('prev-table-body');
  if (previewItems.length === 0) {
    prevTbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;color:#a8a29e;">No items selected.</td></tr>';
  } else {
    prevTbody.innerHTML = previewItems.map(item => `
      <tr>
        <td><strong>${item.name}</strong></td>
        <td style="text-align:center;">${item.qty}</td>
        <td style="text-align:right;">₱${Number(item.price).toFixed(2)}</td>
        <td style="text-align:right;font-weight:bold;">₱${Number(item.subtotal).toFixed(2)}</td>
      </tr>
    `).join('');
  }
}

async function submitInvoice() {
  let customerName = recipientMode === 'db' ? missionaries.find(x => x.email === document.getElementById('missionary-select').value)?.name || 'Missionary' : document.getElementById('custom-name-input').value.trim();
  let customerEmail = recipientMode === 'db' ? document.getElementById('missionary-select').value : document.getElementById('custom-email-input').value.trim();

  if (!customerName) {
    showToast("Please provide customer name.", "error");
    return;
  }

  const items = [];
  let subtotal = 0;
  document.querySelectorAll('.item-row').forEach(r => {
    const name = r.querySelector('.item-select').value;
    const qty = parseInt(r.querySelector('.item-qty').value, 10) || 1;
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.item-price').value) || 0;
    const rowSubtotal = qty * price;
    subtotal += rowSubtotal;
    items.push({ name, qty, price, subtotal: rowSubtotal });
  });

  if (items.length === 0) {
    showToast("Please add items.", "error");
    return;
  }

  const discountType = document.getElementById('discount-type').value;
  const discountVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('discount-val').value) || 0;
  let discountAmount = discountType === 'percent' ? (subtotal * (discountVal / 100)) : discountVal;
  const totalAmount = subtotal - discountAmount;

  const invId = `INV-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: 'create_invoice',
        invoice_id: invId,
        email: customerEmail,
        name: customerName,
        items_json: items,
        subtotal,
        discount_type: discountType,
        discount_val: discountVal,
        discount_amount: discountAmount,
        total_amount: totalAmount
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Invoice ${invId} saved!`);
      loadData();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Error saving invoice", "error");
  }
}

function renderInvoicesTable() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('invoices-tbody');
  document.getElementById('history-badge').textContent = cashInvoices.length;
  if (!cashInvoices.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:20px;color:var(--muted);">No invoices recorded yet.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }
  tbody.innerHTML = cashInvoices.map(inv => `
    <tr>
      <td style="font-weight:bold;color:var(--gold);">${inv.invoice_id}</td>
      <td style="font-weight:600;">${inv.name}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--muted);">${inv.email || '—'}</td>
      <td style="font-weight:bold;">₱${Number(inv.total_amount).toFixed(2)}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--muted);font-size:0.65rem;">${new Date(inv.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
      <td style="text-align:center;"><span style="color:var(--green);font-weight:bold;">${inv.status}</span></td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
}

function renderProductSettings() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('products-settings-tbody');
  tbody.innerHTML = productCatalog.map(p => `
    <tr>
      <td><input type="text" class="form-input p-name" value="${p.name}" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
      <td><input type="number" class="form-input p-price" value="${p.price}" min="0" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
      <td style="text-align:center;"><button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove()">✕</button></td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');
}

function addNewProductSetting() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('products-settings-tbody');
  const tr = document.createElement('tr');
  tr.innerHTML = `
    <td><input type="text" class="form-input p-name" placeholder="New Product Name" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
    <td><input type="number" class="form-input p-price" value="100" min="0" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.72rem;"/></td>
    <td style="text-align:center;"><button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.closest('tr').remove()">✕</button></td>
  `;
  tbody.appendChild(tr);
}

async function saveProductSettings() {
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#products-settings-tbody tr');
  const updated = [];
  rows.forEach(r => {
    const name = r.querySelector('.p-name').value.trim();
    const price = parseFloat(r.querySelector('.p-price').value) || 0;
    if (name) updated.push({ name, price, type: 'cash' });
  });

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'sync_catalog', type: 'cash', products: updated })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast("Cash products saved to Turso!");
      productCatalog = updated;
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Error saving products", "error");
  }
}

function downloadInvoiceImage() {
  html2canvas(document.getElementById('invoice-node'), { scale: 2 }).then(canvas => {
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.download = `Invoice-${Date.now()}.png`;
    link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    link.click();
    showToast("Invoice downloaded!");
  });
}

function copyInvoiceText() {
  const text = `🧾 TIMELESS CREATIONS INVOICE\nID: ${document.getElementById('prev-inv-id').textContent}\nBill To: ${document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent}\nTotal: ${document.getElementById('prev-total').textContent}`;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => showToast("Invoice text copied!"));
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('invoicing', 'Invoicing & POS');
  document.getElementById('prev-date').textContent = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
  loadData();
});

<script>
let allLogs = [];
let currentFilter = 'ALL';

async function loadDetailedLogs() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=get_system_logs&limit=150');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      allLogs = data.logs || [];
      renderLogs();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Failed to fetch system logs", "error");
  }
}

function setFilter(level, btn) {
  currentFilter = level;
  document.querySelectorAll('.filters-bar .filter-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  renderLogs();
}

function renderLogs() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('logs-tbody');
  const query = document.getElementById('log-search').value.toLowerCase().trim();

  let filtered = allLogs.filter(l => {
    const lvl = (l.level || 'INFO').toUpperCase();
    const matchLevel = currentFilter === 'ALL' || lvl === currentFilter;
    const matchQuery = !query || (l.message || '').toLowerCase().includes(query);
    return matchLevel && matchQuery;
  });

  if (filtered.length === 0) {
    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--muted);">No matching log entries found.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map(l => {
    const lvl = (l.level || 'INFO').toUpperCase();
    let badgeCls = 'badge-info';
    if (lvl === 'WARN') badgeCls = 'badge-warn';
    else if (lvl === 'ERROR') badgeCls = 'badge-error';
    else if (lvl === 'TURSO') badgeCls = 'badge-turso';

    const formattedTime = l.created_at ? new Date(l.created_at).toLocaleString() : 'Just now';

    return `
      <tr>
        <td style="color:var(--muted);font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;">#${l.id}</td>
        <td><span class="badge ${badgeCls}">${lvl}</span></td>
        <td style="word-break:break-all;color:var(--text);">${escapeHtml(l.message)}</td>
        <td style="text-align:right;color:var(--muted);font-size:0.68rem;">${formattedTime}</td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  return (str || '').replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('logs', 'System Logs');
  loadDetailedLogs();
  // Auto refresh every 10 seconds for real-time live telemetry
  setInterval(loadDetailedLogs, 10000);
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e

<script>
let currentMonth = 1;
let liveDripMap = {};
let availableRewards = [];

function switchTab(tabId, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach(b => b.classList.remove("active"));
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab-content").forEach(c => c.classList.remove("active"));
  document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add("active");
  btn.classList.add("active");
}

function initSelector() {
  const sel = document.getElementById("month-selector");
  sel.innerHTML = "";
  for (let i = 1; i <= 24; i++) {
    const opt = document.createElement("option");
    opt.value = i;
    opt.textContent = `Month ${i}`;
    sel.appendChild(opt);
  }
}

async function fetchFromTurso() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_drips");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok && Array.isArray(data.drips)) {
      data.drips.forEach(d => {
        liveDripMap[Number(d.month)] = {
          month: Number(d.month),
          theme: d.theme || "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland",
          scripture: d.scripture || "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.",
          message: d.message || `May your faith be strengthened as you serve and invite others to come unto Christ during Month ${d.month}.`,
          highlight_img: d.highlight_img || "",
          highlight_label: d.highlight_label || ""
        };
      });
    }

    const rewRes = await fetch("/api/main?action=get_products&type=reward");
    const rewData = await rewRes.json();
    if (rewData.ok && rewData.products) {
      availableRewards = rewData.products;
    }
  } catch(e) {}
}

function onSelectMonth(m) {
  currentMonth = parseInt(m, 10);
  document.getElementById("unsaved-month-num").textContent = currentMonth;

  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {
    month: currentMonth,
    theme: "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland",
    scripture: "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.",
    message: `May your faith be strengthened as you serve and invite others to come unto Christ during Month ${currentMonth}.`,
    highlight_img: "",
    highlight_label: ""
  };

  document.getElementById("inp-theme").value = data.theme || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-msg").value = data.message || "";
  document.getElementById("inp-quote").value = data.scripture || "";

  if (data.highlight_label && data.highlight_label.trim() !== "") {
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = true;
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = data.highlight_label;
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value = data.highlight_img || "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name-2").value = data.highlight_label_2 || "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img-2").value = data.highlight_img_2 || "";
  } else {
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = false;
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = "";
    document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value = "";
  }

  renderPreview();
}

function onLiveEdit() {
  const isHigh = document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked;
  liveDripMap[currentMonth] = {
    month: currentMonth,
    theme: document.getElementById("inp-theme").value.trim(),
    message: document.getElementById("inp-msg").value.trim(),
    scripture: document.getElementById("inp-quote").value.trim(),
    highlight_label: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value.trim() : "",
    highlight_img: isHigh ? document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value.trim() : ""
  };

  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "flex";
  renderPreview();
}

function cleanQuoteString(raw) {
  if (!raw) return "";
  return raw.replace(/^["'“](.*)["'”]$/, '$1').replace(/^["'“]/, '').replace(/["'”]$/, '').trim();
}

function renderPreview() {
  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {};

  document.getElementById("prev-date").textContent = `Month ${currentMonth} • Dedicated Service`;
  document.getElementById("prev-msg").innerHTML = (data.message || "").replace(/\n/g, "<br>");
  
  const cleanedQuote = cleanQuoteString(data.scripture || "Trust in the Lord with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.");
  document.getElementById("prev-quote").innerHTML = `"${cleanedQuote}"`;
  document.getElementById("prev-speaker").textContent = data.theme || "Elder Jeffrey R. Holland";

  document.getElementById("prev-ess1-name").textContent = document.getElementById("inp-ess1-name").value.trim();
  document.getElementById("prev-ess1-img").src = document.getElementById("inp-img-ess1").value.trim();
  document.getElementById("prev-ess2-name").textContent = document.getElementById("inp-ess2-name").value.trim();
  document.getElementById("prev-ess2-img").src = document.getElementById("inp-img-ess2").value.trim();

  for (let i = 1; i <= 9; i++) {
    const el = document.getElementById(`prev-grid-${i}`);
    const inp = document.getElementById(`inp-grid-${i}`);
    if (el && inp) el.src = inp.value.trim();
  }

  document.getElementById("prev-gallery-link").href = document.getElementById("inp-gallery-url").value.trim();

  const isHigh = document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked;
  const hContainer = document.getElementById("prev-highlight-container");
  if (isHigh && data.highlight_label) {
    hContainer.style.display = "block";
    document.getElementById("prev-highlight-name").textContent = data.highlight_label;
    document.getElementById("prev-highlight-img").src = data.highlight_img || "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/u/0/d/1F7Yb0OzuCmPO2LyZ0cMoaTM4d4rs5RFE";
  } else {
    hContainer.style.display = "none";
  }

  const points = parseInt(document.getElementById("inp-sim-points").value, 10) || 0;
  document.getElementById("prev-points-val").textContent = points;
  const claimBox = document.getElementById("prev-claimable-items");

  const affordable = availableRewards.filter(r => r.price <= points);
  if (affordable.length > 0) {
    claimBox.innerHTML = `
      <div style="font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; color:#1a1a1a; margin-bottom:6px;">🎉 You can claim these reward items right now:</div>
      <div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:6px;">
        ${affordable.map(item => `
          <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; background:#fff; padding:6px 10px; border:1px solid #e0d6bc; border-radius:4px; text-align:left;">
            <img src="${item.image_url || 'https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png'}" style="width:36px; height:36px; object-fit:cover; border-radius:3px; aspect-ratio:1/1;">
            <div style="flex:1;">
              <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size:11px;">${item.name}</div>
              <div style="font-size:9.5px; color:#8c7e5d; font-weight:bold;">⭐ ${item.price} Points</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    `;
  } else {
    const sorted = [...availableRewards].sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price);
    const nearestItem = sorted.find(r => r.price > points) || sorted[0] || { name: "Temple Keychain", price: 6, image_url: "https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png" };
    const needed = Math.max(1, (nearestItem.price || 6) - points);

    claimBox.innerHTML = `
      <div style="background:#fff; border:1px solid #e0d6bc; border-radius:6px; padding:12px; text-align:center;">
        <span style="font-size:10px; color:#8b1a1a; font-weight:bold; letter-spacing:1px; text-transform:uppercase; display:block; margin-bottom:6px;">🔥 You're so close! Next Goal:</span>
        <img src="${nearestItem.image_url || 'https://i.postimg.cc/FFdrCNqq/Untitled56-20260820115353.png'}" style="width:60px; height:60px; object-fit:cover; border-radius:4px; aspect-ratio:1/1; display:block; margin:0 auto 6px auto; border:1px solid #d4c197;">
        <div style="font-weight:bold; font-size:11.5px; color:#1a1a1a;">${nearestItem.name} (${nearestItem.price} PTS)</div>
        <div style="font-size:11px; color:#8c7e5d; margin-top:4px; font-weight:bold;">
          ⚡ Only <strong>${needed} more point${needed > 1 ? 's' : ''}</strong> needed to claim!
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:10px; color:#777; margin-top:4px; line-height:1.3;">
          Invite just <strong>${needed} companion${needed > 1 ? 's' : ''}</strong> with your referral link to unlock this reward!
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }
}

  document.getElementById("inp-highlight-toggle").checked = false;
  document.getElementById("inp-highlight-name").value = "";
  document.getElementById("inp-highlight-img").value = "";
  onLiveEdit();
  showToast("Disabled & reset Product of the Month.");

async function saveActiveMonth() {
  const btn = document.getElementById("btn-main-save");
  const saveBannerBtn = document.getElementById("btn-save-turso");
  const data = liveDripMap[currentMonth] || {};
  
  if (btn) btn.textContent = "⏳ Saving Month to Database...";
  if (saveBannerBtn) saveBannerBtn.textContent = "⏳ Saving...";

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/main", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: "save_drip",
        month: currentMonth,
        theme: data.theme,
        scripture: data.scripture,
        message: data.message,
        highlight_img: data.highlight_img,
        highlight_label: data.highlight_label,
        highlight_label_2: data.highlight_label_2,
        highlight_img_2: data.highlight_img_2
      })
    });
    const resData = await res.json();
    if (resData.ok) {
      document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
      if (btn) btn.textContent = "💾 Save Month to Database";
      if (saveBannerBtn) saveBannerBtn.textContent = "💾 Save Month to Turso";
      showToast(`Month ${currentMonth} saved to database!`);
    } else {
      showToast(resData.error || "Failed to save.", "error");
      if (btn) btn.textContent = "💾 Save Month to Database";
      if (saveBannerBtn) saveBannerBtn.textContent = "💾 Save Month to Turso";
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Network error saving to Turso.", "error");
    if (btn) btn.textContent = "💾 Save Month to Database";
    if (saveBannerBtn) saveBannerBtn.textContent = "💾 Save Month to Turso";
  }
}

window.syncData = async () => {
  document.getElementById("save-banner").style.display = "none";
  await fetchFromTurso();
  onSelectMonth(currentMonth);
  showToast("Re-synced 24 months from Turso database.");
};

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  initAppLayout('drips', '24-Month Drips & Media');
  initSelector();
  await fetchFromTurso();
  onSelectMonth(1);
});

<script>
let parsedCsvData = [];

function toTitleCase(str) {
  if (!str) return '';
  return str.toLowerCase().replace(/(?:^|\s|-)\S/g, char => char.toUpperCase()).trim();
}

function sanitizeEmail(email) {
  if (!email) return '';
  let clean = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (clean && !clean.includes('@')) clean += '@missionary.org';
  return clean;
}

function autoGenerateEmail(row) {
  const emailInput = row.querySelector('.m-email');
  if (emailInput.dataset.manualEdited === "true") return;

  const titleName = row.querySelector('.m-title-name').value.trim();
  const firstName = row.querySelector('.m-first-name').value.trim();
  const cFirst = firstName.toLowerCase().split(/[\s-]+/).filter(Boolean).join('.');
  const cLast = titleName.replace(/^(elder|sister)\s+/i, '').trim().toLowerCase().split(/[\s-]+/).filter(Boolean).join('.');

  if (cFirst && cLast) emailInput.value = `${cFirst}.${cLast}@missionary.org`;
  else if (cLast) emailInput.value = `${cLast}@missionary.org`;
  else emailInput.value = '';
}

function addManualRow() {
  const container = document.getElementById('manual-rows');
  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = 'form-row';
  div.innerHTML = `
    <input type="text" class="form-input m-title-name" placeholder="Elder Dela Cruz" oninput="autoGenerateEmail(this.closest('.form-row'))"/>
    <input type="text" class="form-input m-first-name" placeholder="Mark" oninput="autoGenerateEmail(this.closest('.form-row'))"/>
    <input type="text" class="form-input m-email" placeholder="mark.delacruz@missionary.org" oninput="this.dataset.manualEdited='true'"/>
    <button class="btn-danger" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">✕</button>
  `;
  container.appendChild(div);
}

async function pushManualEntries() {
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.form-row');
  const globalBatch = toTitleCase(document.getElementById('global-batch-input').value.trim()) || 'August 2026';
  const entries = [];

  rows.forEach(r => {
    let titleName = toTitleCase(r.querySelector('.m-title-name').value.trim());
    let firstName = toTitleCase(r.querySelector('.m-first-name').value.trim());
    let email = sanitizeEmail(r.querySelector('.m-email').value.trim());
    if (email && titleName) {
      entries.push({ title_name: titleName, first_name: firstName, email, batch: globalBatch });
    }
  });

  if (entries.length === 0) {
    showToast("Please fill out at least one missionary.", "error");
    return;
  }

  const btn = document.getElementById('push-manual-btn');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = 'Pushing to Turso...';

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=push_missionaries', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ entries })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      showToast(`Pushed ${data.added} missionaries! Tap Global Refresh to sync.`);
      document.getElementById('manual-rows').innerHTML = '';
      addManualRow();
    } else {
      showToast(data.error || "Failed to push", "error");
    }
  } catch(e) {
    showToast("Network error: " + e.message, "error");
  } finally {
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = '🚀 Push Missionaries to Turso';
  }
}

function handleFileSelect(event) {
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  document.getElementById('file-label').textContent = `📄 Selected: ${file.name}`;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    const lines = e.target.result.trim().split(/\r?\n/);
    parsedCsvData = lines.slice(1).map(l => {
      const p = l.split(',').map(s => s.trim().replace(/^["']|['"]$/g, ''));
      return { title_name: p[0], first_name: p[1] || '', email: sanitizeEmail(p[2] || p[1]) };
    }).filter(x => x.title_name && x.email);

    if (parsedCsvData.length > 0) {
      document.getElementById('push-csv-btn').style.display = 'inline-flex';
      showToast(`Parsed ${parsedCsvData.length} records.`);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
}

async function pushCsvEntries() {
  if (parsedCsvData.length === 0) return;
  const res = await fetch('/api/main?action=push_missionaries', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ entries: parsedCsvData })
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  if (data.ok) {
    showToast(`Imported ${data.added} CSV records! Tap Global Refresh to sync.`);
    document.getElementById('push-csv-btn').style.display = 'none';
  }
}

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  initAppLayout('pusher', 'Add Missionaries');
  addManualRow();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

        <button id="upload-btn" onclick="processAndUploadWebP()" style="background:#c9a84c; color:#0d0e15; border:none; padding:12px 20px; border-radius:8px; font-weight:bold; font-size:0.85rem; cursor:pointer; margin-top:14px;">

<script>
let selectedImageFile = null;
let cachedGalleryItems = [];

const dropZone = document.getElementById("drop-zone");
['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach(eName => {
  dropZone.addEventListener(eName, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.add('dragover'); });
});
['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(eName => {
  dropZone.addEventListener(eName, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.remove('dragover'); });
});
dropZone.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
  if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
    setFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
  }
});
window.addEventListener('paste', (e) => {
  const items = (e.clipboardData || e.originalEvent?.clipboardData)?.items;
  if (!items) return;
  for (let item of items) {
    if (item.kind === 'file' && item.type.includes('image/')) {
      setFile(item.getAsFile());
      break;
    }
  }
});

function handleFileSelected(e) {
  if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) setFile(e.target.files[0]);
}

function setFile(file) {
  selectedImageFile = file;
  const meter = document.getElementById("savings-meter");
  meter.style.display = "block";
  meter.textContent = `✓ Selected: ${file.name || 'Pasted Image'} (${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)`;
}

async function processAndUploadWebP() {
  if (!selectedImageFile) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Please select or paste an image first.", "error");
    return;
  }

  const btn = document.getElementById("upload-btn");
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = "⏳ Uploading & Storing in Gallery...";

  const preset = document.getElementById("size-preset-select").value;
  const reader = new FileReader();

  reader.onload = function(event) {
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = async function() {
      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");

      let width = img.width;
      let height = img.height;

      if (preset === "square_600") { width = 600; height = 600; }
      else if (preset === "banner_1200") { width = 1200; height = 630; }
      else if (preset === "thumb_300") { width = 300; height = 300; }

      canvas.width = width;
      canvas.height = height;
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

      const base64Data = canvas.toDataURL("image/webp", 0.85);
      const originalKb = (selectedImageFile.size / 1024).toFixed(1);
      const compressedKb = (Math.round((base64Data.length * 3 / 4)) / 1024).toFixed(1);
      const savedPct = Math.max(0, Math.round((1 - (compressedKb / originalKb)) * 100));

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/cdn?action=upload", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action: "upload",
            filename: selectedImageFile.name || "image.png",
            base64Data,
            targetSize: preset,
            originalKb,
            compressedKb
          })
        });

        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.ok) {
          document.getElementById("savings-meter").textContent = `⚡ Stored in Gallery: ${originalKb}KB → ${compressedKb}KB (${savedPct}% saved!)`;
          if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("✓ Uploaded & stored in Gallery! Direct link copied.");
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(data.direct_url).catch(() => {});
          
          // Prepend newly uploaded item to current gallery
          if (data.item) {
            cachedGalleryItems.unshift(data.item);
            renderGalleryItems(cachedGalleryItems);
          } else {
            loadGallery();
          }
        } else {
          if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(`Upload issue: ${data.error}`, "error");
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Error connecting to CDN API.", "error");
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = "⚡ Convert, Store & Generate Link";
      }
    };
    img.src = event.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(selectedImageFile);
}

async function loadGallery() {
  const container = document.getElementById("gallery-container");
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/cdn?action=list");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok && data.gallery) {
      cachedGalleryItems = data.gallery;
      renderGalleryItems(cachedGalleryItems);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column: 1/-1; text-align:center; color:#8c90a4; padding:40px;">Failed to load CDN gallery.</div>';
  }
}

function renderGalleryItems(items) {
  const container = document.getElementById("gallery-container");
  if (!items || items.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column: 1/-1; text-align:center; color:#8c90a4; padding:40px;">No images stored yet. Upload one above!</div>';
    return;
  }

  container.innerHTML = items.map(item => `
    <div class="gallery-item">
      <img src="${item.direct_url}" class="gallery-thumb" alt="${item.filename}" onclick="openLightbox('${item.direct_url}')" loading="lazy">
      <div style="padding:10px; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:6px;">
        <div style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace; font-size:0.68rem; color:#e8e6f0; font-weight:bold; white-space:nowrap; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis;" title="${item.filename}">
          🔒 ${item.filename}
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:0.65rem; color:#8c90a4;">Preset: ${item.size_label || 'WebP 85%'}</div>
        <button class="btn-action btn-gold-action" onclick="copyUrl('${item.direct_url}')">📋 Copy Direct Link</button>
        <button class="btn-action btn-drip-action" onclick="insertIntoDrips('${item.direct_url}')">💌 Insert into Drips</button>
        <button class="btn-action btn-del-action" onclick="deleteImage(${item.id})">🗑️ Remove</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function filterGallery() {
  const q = document.getElementById("gallery-search").value.toLowerCase();
  const filtered = cachedGalleryItems.filter(i => (i.filename || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (i.size_label || '').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  renderGalleryItems(filtered);
}

function copyUrl(url) {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(() => {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("📋 Direct link copied to clipboard!");
  });
}

function insertIntoDrips(url) {
  sessionStorage.setItem("tcrp_inserted_image_url", url);
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("✓ Direct link saved! Opening Drip Editor...");
  setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = "/drips.html"; }, 600);
}

function openLightbox(url) {
  document.getElementById("lightbox-img").src = url;
  document.getElementById("lightbox-modal").style.display = "flex";
}

function closeLightbox() {
  document.getElementById("lightbox-modal").style.display = "none";
}

async function deleteImage(id) {
  if (!confirm("Remove this asset from the gallery store?")) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/cdn?action=delete", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete", id })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.ok) {
      if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Asset purged from gallery store.");
      loadGallery();
    }
  } catch(e) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast("Failed to delete.", "error");
  }
}

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  if (typeof initAppLayout === 'function') initAppLayout('gallery', 'CDN Media Gallery');
  loadGallery();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
-e
